The 3 reference files (flow spreadsheet + 2 real customer billing-note PDFs) were originally dropped
in `public/` (served as public static assets in production — a real data-exposure risk, since they
contain real customer billing data) and have since been **moved to `reference/accounting/` +
gitignored (2026-08-17)** so they can't end up committed/deployed by accident.

Exploratory conversation only so far — no design decisions made, nothing built. Accounting department
has already **purchased pre-printed multi-part NCR (carbonless copy) continuous forms** covering 4
document types in one set: ใบแจ้งหนี้/ใบวางบิล (Invoice/Billing Note), สำเนาใบเสร็จรับเงิน (Receipt
copy), สำเนาใบกำกับภาษี (Tax Invoice copy), and ใบส่งสินค้า (Delivery Note). They intend to print onto
these using a **dot-matrix (impact) printer** — a hard requirement, not a preference: only mechanical
impact transfers ink through the carbon layers to the copies underneath; laser/inkjet only print the
top sheet, leaving the copies blank.

- [x] ~~**Scope gap: 3 of the 4 document types don't exist in the ERP at all.**~~ — **ล้าสมัยแล้ว,
  ปิดเมื่อ 2026-09-04.** ข้อความเดิมเขียนไว้ก่อนโมดูลบัญชี Phase 1/1.5 ว่า "ใบแจ้งหนี้/ใบวางบิล,
  ใบเสร็จรับเงิน และใบกำกับภาษี ยังไม่มี data model / workflow / UI เลย" — **ไม่จริงตั้งแต่
  2026-08-18** ทั้งสี่ชนิดมีจริงแล้วเป็น `ArDocumentType = "AR" | "IV" | "BI" | "RE"`
  (`src/lib/accounting.ts:44`) พร้อมหน้าเมนูของตัวเองชนิดละหน้า การออกเลขแบบ atomic และใบพิมพ์
  ทั้งแบบกระดาษเปล่าและแบบ NCR · ใบส่งสินค้ายังเป็นโมดูลใบส่งมอบสินค้าที่พิมพ์เลย์เอาต์ของตัวเอง
  ไม่ใช่ฟอร์ม NCR ตามเดิม — ส่วนนั้นยังค้างอยู่จริง แต่เป็นคนละเรื่องกับ "ยังไม่มีเอกสารในระบบ"
  งานที่เหลือของหมวดนี้อยู่ในข้อคาลิเบรตเครื่องพิมพ์และข้อฟิลด์ที่ฟอร์มต้องการด้านล่าง
- [ ] **The physical print-calibration problem — software side now built (2026-08-18), hardware
  iteration still required.** The data-only NCR print mode exists (`ArDocumentNcrPrintDocument.tsx`:
  absolute-mm fields, one page per carbon set, per-machine page-size/offset settings, a crosshair
  test page — see CHANGELOG.md 2026-08-18c), with first-draft coordinates derived proportionally
  from the owner's photos of the real forms. **Remaining, and impossible in code alone**: get the
  real form's dimensions (assumed 9"×11" continuous — unconfirmed), print the test page from the
  actual dot-matrix printer onto real form stock, measure, adjust the offsets in "ตั้งค่าฟอร์ม NCR"
  (and `FORM` in the component if individual fields are off rather than the whole page), and repeat
  until the `+` marks land on the boxes. Connecting the dot-matrix printer is just an OS-level
  printer target for `window.print()` — not a web-app concern.
- [x] ~~**Confirm with the owner whether the Billing Note (BI/ใบแจ้งหนี้/ใบวางบิล) form is also
  pre-purchased NCR stock**~~ — **confirmed 2026-08-18, same day**: the owner sent a photo of the
  actual blank pre-printed BI form stock ("ตัวใบแจ้งหนี้มันมาเป็นฟอร์มเปล่าด้วย") — it IS NCR stock
  like AR/IV/RE, not plain paper as first guessed. Built the matching data-only NCR mode the same
  day: `BillingNoteNcrPage` + `FORM_BI` in `ArDocumentNcrPrintDocument.tsx`, reusing the existing
  "NCR" print button + calibration dialog/test page (now with a `variant` prop so the test page can
  target either form family). **`FORM_BI`'s coordinates are still a first-draft best-effort guess**
  (no measured photo of this specific form's field positions was provided, unlike AR/IV/RE which had
  one) — needs the same physical calibration pass before real use. Live-verified via print-CSS-
  emulation screenshot (structure/fields render correctly) — see CHANGELOG.md 2026-08-18k.
- [ ] **NCR form fields the ERP doesn't store yet — decide whether to add them.** The real form
  prints 3 fields `ArDocument` has no data for (currently left blank in NCR mode): **customer code**
  (e.g. `H-020` — `Customer.code` exists since Phase 1 but isn't carried into
  `ArDocument.customerSnapshot`), **สถานที่ส่งสินค้า / Place To Delivery**, and **ผู้ขาย / Sale**
  (e.g. `SK-สุนันท์ ขำมี` — the Scope of Work's salesperson would be the natural source). Adding them
  means extending the snapshot at issue time (new documents only) — worth doing before real NCR use
  if accounting needs those boxes filled.
- [ ] **Open questions for the accounting department before starting**: exact dot-matrix printer
  make/model (affects paper-size/driver setup), whether the printer is already on hand or still being
  procured (blocks calibration until it exists), and the exact form dimensions (continuous-feed width,
  likely ~9.5") plus a measurement of each field's position on a real blank form.

  Draft saves + re-checks uniqueness; a Final record's number field is genuinely read-only;
  Duplicate's prompt → new number → new record flow; Rewrite of a manually-numbered record
  produces `{typed}-R1`; old auto-numbered records still list/search/open normally.

  `customerPoNumber`/`documentRecipients`/`documentRecipientMessage` (`FOLLOW_UP_FIELDS`) +

Optional CSV export of real dashboard KPIs would be a reasonable low-risk future add if requested.

- [ ] **`App.tsx` never re-checks the session after initial boot, so a user whose cookie has since expired/gone invalid sees no warning until an action actually fails** (2026-07-21, from a real user report of a confusing "Not authenticated" toast on Approve — see CHANGELOG.md "Translate the raw 'Not authenticated' 401 into a real Thai/English message"). That pass only fixed the *message text* (now "เซสชันหมดอายุ กรุณาเข้าสู่ระบบใหม่" instead of the raw English server string); the UI still shows the user as logged in and lets them keep clicking things that will all fail the same way until they manually log out/in. A real fix would need either a periodic/on-focus session re-check in `App.tsx` that redirects to Sign In the moment a session goes invalid, or a global 401 handler in `apiClient.ts` that forces sign-out UI immediately on any "Not authenticated" response — neither built yet, flag to the user before doing either since both are bigger than a message fix.

## Medium Priority — Production-readiness follow-ups (2026-07-09 request)

- [x] **Translate the rest of the app's Thai UI** — **done 2026-07-09 (follow-up pass)**: sidebar/topbar, login/setup pages, Settings (all tabs), Products, Quotations (screen editing UI), User/Role Management, and Audit Log are now all wired to `useI18n()`/`t()` (~450 dictionary keys). Introduced a stable `NavKey` enum decoupled from the (now-translatable) display label, since navigation state was previously keyed off the Thai label text itself — a real bug that would have broken routing once labels became translatable. Two things remain intentionally Thai-only, not gaps: persisted data/seed content (audit log, notifications, role/department descriptions, company defaults) and `PrintDocument.tsx` (the actual printed/PDF quotation — a real business document, not app chrome). See [CLAUDE.md](./CLAUDE.md) Coding Standards.
- [ ] **Confirm `product_templates` collection semantics.** Added as schema-only scaffolding with a starting interpretation (reusable presets for fast product creation) — not confirmed with the user, don't build UI against it without checking first. See [DATABASE.md](./DATABASE.md).
- [ ] **Replace the generic department/position seed list** (ฝ่ายขาย/ฝ่ายจัดซื้อ/ฝ่ายคลังสินค้า/ฝ่ายบัญชี/ฝ่ายทรัพยากรบุคคล/ฝ่ายบริหาร/ฝ่ายไอที; พนักงาน/หัวหน้างาน/ผู้จัดการ/ผู้จัดการทั่วไป/กรรมการผู้จัดการ) with the company's real org structure once known, then build an admin UI for managing them and wire `User.department`/`User.position` (currently free text) to reference these collections instead.
- [ ] Build API routes + UI on top of the other 2026-07-09 schema-prep collections as their features come up: `quotation_comments`, `quotation_tags`, `permissions` (admin-configurable permission registry), `sessions` (session revocation / "log out other devices"), `uploads`/`attachments` (real blob storage, would also fix the base64-in-document 16MB ceiling noted below).
- [x] ~~Re-verify the MongoDB Atlas credential rotation item above is actually closed — it predates this pass and remains unconfirmed.~~ — **moot as of the ~2026-08-07 server migration, confirmed 2026-08-17**: see the resolved item above (High Priority section) — Atlas isn't used in production anymore at all.

## Medium Priority — Completion-audit follow-ups (2026-07-10 request)

- [ ] **Activity Analytics** (period-grouped, multi-dimension) — a genuine gap surfaced by the 2026-07-10 completion audit (see [IMPLEMENTATION_CHECKLIST.md](./IMPLEMENTATION_CHECKLIST.md)). The Dashboard's Activity Timeline is a flat recent-N feed from `audit_log`; there's no weekly/monthly/quarterly/yearly grouping or salesperson/job-type filtering of activity counts (quotations created/edited/won/lost, status changes, approvals). Not yet scoped — reasonably buildable from existing `audit_log` + `quotes` data (audit log entries carry the actor's identity for a salesperson-style filter; job-type filtering of *activity* would need cross-referencing `details` text or isn't well-supported by the current audit log shape, worth scoping explicitly before building).
- [ ] Build a Job Type admin management UI — the `POST`/`PATCH /api/jobtypes` routes exist (`company:manage`), but there's no page; only the 13 seeded defaults are usable today.

## Medium Priority — Executive Dashboard follow-ups (2026-07-10 request)

- [x] ~~Report Export CSV for the Dashboard~~ — **done 2026-07-10** (Codex review fix pass): client-side CSV export (`src/pages/dashboard/csvExport.ts`, no new dependency) of KPIs + Sales Performance/Top Customers/Job Type Analytics tables, built from the already-filtered `DashboardStats` already on screen. "Export CSV" button in the Dashboard header.
- [ ] **Report Export — PDF** for the Dashboard still open (CSV done above; Excel done 2026-07-24 and **rebuilt as the detailed 10-sheet Thai workbook 2026-09-07** — `src/pages/dashboard/reportRows.ts` is the single row source for both Excel and CSV; see [MODULES/Dashboard.md](./MODULES/Dashboard.md) "Excel / CSV export — detailed workbook"). PDF should extend the existing browser-print pattern (`PrintDocument.tsx` precedent) rather than adding a heavy new dependency.
- [ ] **Confirm `MIN_STAGE_SAMPLE = 5` with the business (2026-09-07).** The closing-probability sheet refuses to print a percentage for a pipeline stage with fewer than 5 closed quotations in the trailing 12 months and falls back to the company win rate instead. Five is a guess at "enough not to mislead"; if the owner wants stage percentages sooner (or later), change the constant in `api/_lib/dashboardAnalytics.ts` — one line, tests parameterise it.
- [ ] Real Lead/Customer entities (see the Lead & Customer Management item above) would resolve the Dashboard's free-text `client`-string-matching caveat for repeat-customer/top-customer analytics, and let the sales pipeline start at "Lead" instead of "Draft."
- [ ] A dedicated monthly quotation-*count* aggregation (as opposed to reusing the monthly *revenue* series) would make `QuotationTrendChart` show real data instead of an approximation.
- [ ] Confirm whether cross-salesperson rankings/performance data on the Dashboard should be restricted for the Sales User role (currently visible to any `dashboard:view` holder, same as every other KPI) — not built, no requirement for it yet, would need a new permission if requested.
- [ ] Build a Job Type admin management UI (the `POST`/`PATCH /api/jobtypes` routes exist, gated by `company:manage`, but there's no dedicated page — only the 13 seeded defaults are usable today, no way to add a 14th via the UI).
- [ ] **Persist a canonical pre-VAT quotation total at write time** instead of reconstructing it via `amount / 1.07` on every Dashboard request — flagged Medium by the 2026-07-14 Codex review. Correct for all current data (no intermediate rounding, `VAT_RATE` always a fixed 7%), but fragile if VAT ever becomes quote-specific or the rate changes historically. Needs a new persisted field (`subtotal`/`amountBeforeVat`) plus write-path changes in `api/handlers/quotes.ts`/`quoteValidation.ts` and a backfill decision for existing quotes — a schema migration, not a quick Dashboard fix.
- [ ] **Replace free-text salesperson/department matching with real IDs for Dashboard/activity filtering** — flagged Medium by the 2026-07-14 Codex review (same underlying gap as the pre-existing free-text-join caveats in MODULES/Dashboard.md). `Quote.salesperson`/`User.department` are free text; Sales Activity's `userName`-based actor attribution means a manager approving someone else's quote gets credited to the manager, not the salesperson. Needs `salespersonId`/`departmentId` fields referencing `User` on `Quote` and audit-log entries — a Quotation-module schema change, out of a Dashboard-only pass's scope.
- [ ] **Bound the Sales Activity Analytics audit-log query even when no date filter is selected** — flagged Medium (performance) by the 2026-07-14 Codex review. The 2026-07-14 fix bounds the query by `createdAt` whenever `from`/`to` is set, but the default (no filter) view still scans full audit-log history before bucketing/zero-filling in Node — same rolling-window convention `revenueTrend` already uses. A hard floor (e.g. cap at the widest displayed window, 5 years back) plus a MongoDB-side aggregation instead of in-Node bucketing would scale better as audit-log history grows.

## Medium Priority — Quotation Templates Codex review follow-ups (2026-07-14 request)

Zero Critical + 3 High Priority issues from this review were fixed the same day (template↔Job
Type server-side binding, 2 missing Excel source rows restored, 9 discarded real literal values
reclassified to specifications — see CHANGELOG.md). The following Medium items were explicitly
left unaddressed, deliberately out of scope for a "fix Critical/High" pass:

- [x] ~~No admin UI for Quotation Template management.~~ **Done 2026-07-15** — full `จัดการ Template ใบเสนอราคา` module (list + create/edit/duplicate/activate/archive), see CHANGELOG.md.
- [x] ~~`POST /api/quotation-templates/import` doesn't parse or hash the actual `.xlsx` file.~~ **Partially done 2026-07-15 (second fix pass)** — `api/_lib/templateWorkbookParser.ts` now really reads the workbook and hashes each sheet's content (`sourceWorkbookHash`), so a workbook edit is now genuinely detectable (real import warnings). **Still open**: the parser doesn't auto-classify parsed rows into `TemplateSection[]`/`TemplateItem[]` — `templateSeedData.ts` still needs manual re-transcription when a warning fires. Deliberately deferred (not an oversight) — direct inspection of the real workbook found rows that pack multiple different classifications into different columns of one row, too ambiguous to safely automate without risking silent content corruption. See MODULES/QuotationTemplates.md "Real Workbook Change Detection" for the concrete evidence and CODEX_REVIEW_REPORT.md "Claude Fix Status."
- [x] ~~Quote template snapshot is flattened, not structured.~~ **Done 2026-07-15 (second fix pass)** — `Quote.templateSnapshot` now holds a real, frozen-at-creation copy of the matched template's `sections`/`defaultTerms`/`internalNotes`/`sourceHash`, alongside the existing `quotationTemplateId/Name/Version` provenance strings. See MODULES/QuotationTemplates.md "Structured Template Snapshot."
- [x] ~~Template import is find-then-insert/update, not a single atomic upsert.~~ **Partially done 2026-07-15 (second fix pass)** — a concurrent-insert race no longer surfaces as an unhandled duplicate-key error (caught and treated as "skipped"). Still not a single atomic `findOneAndUpdate` — deliberately, to preserve the tested "zero writes when unchanged" guarantee an always-`$set` upsert would break. A genuinely concurrent *content-changing* race remains last-write-wins, an accepted edge case for a low-frequency admin action.
- [ ] **No real version-history/rollback for Quotation Templates.** `version` (added 2026-07-14, still true after both 2026-07-15 passes) is a plain string an admin edits by hand — there's no way to browse or restore a template's prior content. Quote-level snapshotting (now genuinely structured, see above) already guarantees existing quotations never change when a master template is edited, so this is a "nice to have" for template authoring history, not a correctness gap. Would need a `quotation_template_versions` collection (or embedded version history array) plus editor UI to browse/restore.
- [ ] **Full automated classification of parsed workbook rows into structured template content.** See the partially-done import item above — real parsing/hashing exists, but turning raw rows into `TemplateSection[]`/`TemplateItem[]` automatically (replacing hand-transcription) remains deliberately unbuilt given the demonstrated row-level ambiguity in the real workbook. Only worth building if manual re-transcription becomes a real recurring bottleneck, and should be done carefully, sheet by sheet, verified against current hand-transcribed content as ground truth.
- [ ] **Live browser/DB verification of the Quotation Templates feature (both the 2026-07-15 Template Management pass and its same-day second fix pass).** `tsc`/`lint`/`build` pass clean on both and a local Playwright check confirmed zero console errors on both, but no local MongoDB credentials means the actual list/create/edit/duplicate/archive/import/apply-to-quotation flows have never round-tripped against a real `quotation_templates`/`quotes` collection. Run the task spec's own manual test plan against a real deployment.

## Medium Priority — สต๊อกบาร์โค้ด: ยิงปืนแล้วเข้าระบบเลย (สำรวจแล้ว 2026-08-28, รอเจ้าของตัดสินใจ)

- [ ] **ทำสต๊อกเป็นบาร์โค้ด ยิงปืนแล้วเข้าระบบเลย — ทำได้ ยังไม่เริ่ม (2026-08-28, เจ้าของถาม).**
  เจ้าของถามว่า *"ถ้าจะทำสต๊อกเป็นบาร์โค้ดสามารถทำได้ไหม ถ้าแบบใช้ปืนยิงละเข้าระบบเลย"* แล้วบอกว่า
  **"อย่าพึ่งทำนะ ถามเฉย ๆ"** — จดไว้เพื่อไม่ให้ต้องไปสำรวจโค้ดใหม่ทั้งหมดถ้ากลับมาถามอีก

  **คำตอบ: ทำได้ และไม่ต้องลง npm เพิ่มสักตัว** — ปืนยิงบาร์โค้ด USB เป็นอุปกรณ์ HID คือเสียบแล้ว
  ทำตัวเป็น *คีย์บอร์ด* ยิงแล้วมัน "พิมพ์" ตัวเลขรัว ๆ ให้เองแล้วกด Enter ปิดท้าย ไม่ต้องลงไดรเวอร์
  ไม่ต้องเขียนตัวเชื่อมต่ออะไรทั้งนั้น สิ่งเดียวที่ต้องเขียนคือตัวแยกว่า *ชุดคีย์ที่มาเร็วผิดมนุษย์
  (< 30 ms ต่อตัว) แล้วปิดท้ายด้วย Enter = การยิง ไม่ใช่การพิมพ์*

  **ขอบเขตที่เจ้าของเลือกไว้แล้ว** (ตอบไว้ 2026-08-28): ยิงตอน **รับของ** (⚠️ ใบตรวจรับถูกถอดออก
  2026-08-28d — ต้องคุยใหม่ว่าจะยิงลงเอกสารไหน) · **นับสต๊อก** ·
  **เบิก/ตัดสต๊อก** · บาร์โค้ด **ต้องพิมพ์ฉลากเอง** (ของส่วนใหญ่ไม่มีติดมา) · ใช้ **ทั้งปืน USB และกล้องมือถือ**

  ส่วนนี้คือของที่มีค่าที่สุดของบันทึกนี้ ถ้าไม่มีต้องไปไล่โค้ดใหม่ทั้งหมด
  - **`Product.code` เอามาเป็นบาร์โค้ดตรง ๆ ไม่ได้** — แอดมินแก้ได้ตลอดเวลา (`api/handlers/products.ts:71`)
    และ **ไม่มี unique index ในฐานข้อมูลจริงเลย** กันซ้ำด้วย `findOne` ที่ `api/handlers/products.ts:31`

    ต้องใช้ functional setState ทุกจุด และต้องแก้ผ่าน setter เดิม (`setDraft`/`setLine`) ไม่งั้น auto-save เพี้ยน
  - **iPhone/Safari ยิงด้วยกล้องไม่ได้** ถ้าไม่ลงไลบรารีอ่านภาพ (เช่น zxing ~200–500 KB) —
    Android/Chrome ใช้ `BarcodeDetector` ที่ฝังมาในเบราว์เซอร์ได้ฟรี · เสนอให้ iPhone พิมพ์รหัสเองไปก่อน
  - **เน็ตหลุด = ยิงไม่ได้** — มี `public/manifest.webmanifest` (ติดตั้งลงหน้าจอได้) แต่ **ไม่มี service worker**
    ทุกครั้งที่ยิงต้องคุยกับเซิร์ฟเวอร์ ถ้าสัญญาณในสโตร์ไม่ดีต้องคิดเรื่องนี้แยกต่างหาก

- [ ] **[Reference, not a gap] Confirmed 2026-08-17: Global Search already has anti-overload safeguards — no action needed.** Came up in conversation (owner asking whether live-search-as-you-type would hammer the server on every keystroke). Checked the actual code: `src/components/GlobalSearch.tsx` debounces 300ms, requires ≥2 typed characters before firing, and cancels any still-in-flight request via `AbortController` when a newer keystroke arrives; `api/_lib/searchHandler.ts` additionally caps query length server-side at 100 chars. If a future search UI is added elsewhere (e.g. inside the not-yet-built Accounting module below), reuse this exact pattern rather than reinventing it.
- [ ] **[Reference, not scoped] Load balancing / multi-replica failover — discussed 2026-08-17, explicitly just curiosity, no current need.** Owner asked about the "3 servers, if one dies the other 2 take over" pattern seen in a video (standard load-balancer + health-check failover). Explained: would need (1) multiple app instances, (2) a load balancer in front (nginx, already in the stack, could do this via an `upstream` block) with health checks, (3) MongoDB replica set for the DB tier specifically (data must actually sync, not just run independent copies). **Not pursued** — production today is a single VPS with one instance each of nginx/app/MongoDB, no redundancy at all, and that's fine for a single-company internal tool unless real downtime becomes a business problem. Revisit only if that changes.
- [ ] Dark mode — `theme.css` only defines the light palette currently; Tailwind's `dark:` variant isn't wired up.
- [ ] Revisit `DashboardPage` bundle size (~502KB gzipped ~132KB as of the second 2026-07-10 pass, up from ~490KB, mostly `recharts`) if more chart-heavy modules get added.
- [ ] Base64-in-document uploads (logo/stamp/profile picture/signature) have a practical 16MB MongoDB document ceiling — the `uploads`/`attachments` collections (schema-only, 2026-07-09) are prep for a future real blob-storage migration.
- [ ] **Revisit the image upload byte caps now that client-side compression (2026-08-14) is cheap.** `MAX_IMAGE_BYTES`/`MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES`/`MAX_ATTACHMENT_BYTES` (and Service's photo caps) were sized when every upload stored its original, uncompressed bytes; now that `compressImageFile()` (`src/lib/imageCompression.ts`) runs before every cap check, the same caps admit noticeably higher-resolution originals than before. Worth deciding whether to raise any cap now that a "1MB" limit typically represents a much larger pre-compression photo than it used to — not changed this pass, since the caps still work correctly as-is and no user has reported hitting one post-compression.
- [ ] **Verify Sales User's production role actually has `deliveryOrder:view/create/edit/print`.** Noticed in passing 2026-07-29 while granting the 4 other pending Role Management permissions (see CHANGELOG.md "Granted the 4 pending permissions") — Sales User's permission count (8) wasn't inspected in detail, and its own Delivery Order grant predates this pass by the same `defaultRoles`-only-seeds-once gap that affected the other roles, but it wasn't in the specific 4-item list actually verified this pass. A Super Admin should open Role Management → Sales User and confirm the 4 Delivery Order boxes are checked; if not, tick them.
- [ ] The per-page guided tour (`useModuleTour`, added 2026-07-29) auto-fires on a user's first visit to a page and repeatedly obscured screenshots while refreshing the user manual's images (2026-07-29) — worth a documented way to capture clean screenshots (dismiss via Escape before shooting, or seed the tour's per-user "seen" flag) if screenshotting the app becomes a recurring task.
- [ ] `CustomerAnalytics.tsx`'s stat-tile caption sits at `text-[10px]` in plain sentence case, which doesn't qualify for `DESIGN.md`'s uppercase/tracking-wide "section eyebrow" exception to the 12px chrome floor. Caught by the `/impeccable critique` detector pass (2026-07-29) on `ApprovalDashboard.tsx` and confirmed as a real (if minor) drift; the owner explicitly scoped it out of that day's fix pass. Trivial fix: `text-[10px]` → `text-xs`, matching `ExecutiveSummaryCards.tsx`'s identical caption pattern. **Same drift also at `src/App.tsx:865`** (the topbar role name, `text-[10px] font-mono`, likewise not uppercase/tracking-wide) — found by the design hook 2026-08-20 while working on a different file, left alone because it sits in the topbar user block where a size change affects truncation/width and no browser was available to verify it visually. Fix both together in one pass. **A third batch of the same class surfaced 2026-08-25** when the design hook ran over the unsaved-changes-guard commit: ~28 uses of `text-[11px]` (also off the 12px chrome floor, and not the uppercase-eyebrow exception) concentrated in `ServiceReportEditor.tsx` (inline field-error `<p>` tags), `ScopeOfWorkDocument.tsx`, `QuoteDocument.tsx:884`, and one each in `JobOrderDocument.tsx`, `MaterialRequisitionDocument.tsx` and `PurchaseRequestDocument.tsx`. All of it `git blame`s to 2026-07-23 / 2026-08-06 / 2026-08-07 — none was introduced by that commit, which added no `text-[11px]` and no `<img>` at all — so it was left alone as pre-existing, per DESIGN.md's Scope-discipline rule against redesigning unrelated pages while in the area. Roll it into the same pass. Note the hook also reported a `broken-image` at `ServiceReportEditor.tsx:88`, which is a **false positive**: that line is `customerSignatureDataUrl: report.customerSignatureDataUrl ?? ""`, a defensive default whose own comment explains it exists precisely so a stale cached response cannot render a broken `<img>`.

- [ ] **Decide whether the light-surface scroll regions should get a styled scrollbar too (opened 2026-08-26).** The sidebar's rail now uses `.sidebar-scroll` (`src/styles/index.css`) because a white OS track down a navy column is genuinely glaring; every other scroll region in the app — page body, tables inside `overflow-x-auto`, the dropdown panels, long dialogs — still renders the OS default, which on Windows is a ~17px track with arrow buttons on white/paper-blue. That's unremarkable rather than ugly, which is why it was deliberately left out of the sidebar fix rather than swept in. Worth one decision: either leave it (OS-native everywhere except the one dark surface that needs help) or mint a light-surface twin of `.sidebar-scroll` using `--muted-foreground`/`--border` ink and apply it globally. Don't do it piecemeal — half-styled scrollbars read worse than none.

- [ ] **`.impeccable/design.json` is stale — regenerate before trusting the design hook's font-size findings (2026-08-25).** The sidecar dates from 2026-07-29 while `DESIGN.md` was last edited 2026-08-25 (commit `50bfd06`), so the hook is checking against an older type ramp than the documented one. Run `/impeccable document` to refresh it, then re-run `/impeccable audit` — some current findings may resolve on their own, and any that survive are worth acting on.
- [ ] `ApprovalDashboard.tsx`'s `confirmReject()` doesn't reset `busy` to `false` on its success path (only in the `catch` branch) — unlike `approve()`, which uses a `finally`. Noticed 2026-07-29 during the Approve-confirmation hardening pass; not currently observable because a successful reject triggers `onRefresh()`, which removes the row (and thus the component) from the list before the stale `busy=true` could matter. Worth a `finally` for consistency/future-proofing if this component is touched again.

- [x] **[2026-08-25] Refresh no longer flashes Settings — the real half of a fix that had been signed off twice.**
  ผู้ใช้แจ้งซ้ำว่า *"กดรีเฟรชละมันยังเห็นไปตั้งค่าละเด้งกลับมาหน้าเดิมอยู่เลย"*. การแก้เมื่อ 2026-08-17c
  กั้นเฉพาะ effect ที่เขียน hash จึงหยุดได้แค่ **URL** แต่ `effectiveNav` ถูกคำนวณใหม่ทุก render และ
  ไม่ได้ถูกกั้น — ระหว่างที่ `roles` ยังเป็น `[]` ทุก `hasPermission()` ตอบว่าไม่มีสิทธิ์ `homeNav` เลย
  ตกไปที่ `"settings"` แล้ว **หน้าตั้งค่าก็ถูกวาดจริง ๆ** ชั่วขณะหนึ่ง. ย้ายการตัดสินใจออกมาเป็น
  `src/lib/navResolution.ts` (`decided: false` ตราบใดที่ยังไม่รู้สิทธิ์ และคงหน้าที่ URL ขอไว้) และให้
  `App.tsx` แสดง `SectionLoading` แทนการวาดหน้าใด ๆ ในช่วงนั้น. เพิ่ม `tests/navResolution.test.ts`
  7 เทสต์ **ทดสอบแล้วว่าจับได้จริง** ด้วยการปิดการแก้แล้วดูว่าแดง 3 ข้อ. ดู CHANGELOG.md 2026-08-25e

- [x] **[2026-08-25] 🔴 แก้ที่ต้นเหตุแล้ว: ไม่มี JSX เหลืออยู่ใน import chain ที่เซิร์ฟเวอร์โหลดจริง + มีเทสต์กันพลาดถาวร.**
  ปิดสองข้อพร้อมกัน (ข้อ 🔴 เดิม กับข้อ "ควรทำเช็คอัตโนมัติ"). ตอนไล่ import จริงพบว่าเหลือ **จุดเดียว**
  ที่ทำให้เซิร์ฟเวอร์ต้องโหลดไฟล์ `.tsx` คือ `api/_lib/arHandler.ts` → `import { bahtText }` ที่เหลือเป็น
  `import type` ทั้งหมด ซึ่งถูกลบทิ้งตอนคอมไพล์อยู่แล้ว **สิ่งที่ทำ**: ย้าย `statusIcon` ไป
  `src/pages/quotation/statusIcons.tsx`, ย้าย `bahtText` ไป `src/lib/bahtText.ts` (ไฟล์เปล่า ๆ ไม่ import
  อะไรเลย), แล้วเปลี่ยน `src/lib/quotes.tsx` → `src/lib/quotes.ts` เป็น TypeScript ล้วน. `quotes.ts`
  re-export `bahtText` ต่อ ที่เรียกใช้ฝั่งหน้าเว็บจึงไม่ต้องแก้เลย. เพิ่ม `tests/serverImportGraph.test.ts`
  ที่เดิน import graph จาก `api/` จริง ๆ (ข้าม `import type`) แล้วฟ้องถ้าเจอ `.tsx` หรือการ import แพ็กเกจ
  React — **ทดสอบแล้วว่ามันจับได้จริง** ด้วยการแกล้งใส่ import กลับเข้าไปแล้วดูว่าเทสต์แดง. ผลพลอยได้:
  คำเตือน lint ลดจาก 35 เหลือ 3 (กฎ `react-refresh/only-export-components` ไม่จับไฟล์ `.ts`).
  ตรวจในเบราว์เซอร์จริงแล้วว่าไอคอนสถานะยังขึ้นครบทั้งหน้ารายการและหน้าเอกสาร และคำอ่านจำนวนเงินภาษาไทย
  ในเอกสารพิมพ์ยังถูกต้อง (`฿1,140,016.41` → "(หนึ่งล้านหนึ่งแสนสี่หมื่นสิบหกบาทสี่สิบเอ็ดสตางค์)") รวมถึงยิง
  `/api/ar-documents` เพื่อบังคับให้ฝั่งเซิร์ฟเวอร์โหลด `arHandler.ts` จริง → 200. ดู CHANGELOG.md 2026-08-25b

- [x] **[2026-08-25] Auto-save on every document editor + discounts enterable in baht.** Owner

  ให้ทุกเอกสารเลย"* and *"เปอร์เซ็นต์ส่วนลดช่วยระบุเป็นจำนวนเงินเลยได้ไหมไม่ต้องเป็นเปอร์เซ็นต์"*. Shared
  `src/hooks/useAutoSave.ts` (local `localStorage` snapshot + silent Draft-only server PATCH) wired
  into Quotation, Scope of Work, Delivery Order, Material Requisition, Job Order, Purchase Request,
  Production Order and Service Report; `?autoSave=1` / `isAutoSaveRequest()` suppresses the audit
  entry and gates to Drafts server-side. `discountMode: "percent" | "amount"` on `Quote`/`QuoteLine`
  (optional, absent = percent, no migration), with the money math consolidated into the single
  React-free `src/lib/quoteMath.ts` that `api/_lib/quoteAmounts.ts` re-exports. Verified live in the
  running app; see CHANGELOG.md 2026-08-25.
 (`2026-08-20-stock-and-manual-tax-invoice`), together with the new one for the whole ผลิต department (`2026-08-20-production-department`). Both written in Thai for staff, covering what the feature does rather than how it was built.

- [x] **[2026-08-20] Resolved: Quotation's product picker no longer surfaces the 82 internal-only
  store SKUs — category-level filter, decided over a per-product flag.** The Material
  Requisition/Purchase Request product pickers had been correctly filtered down to just the 4
  material categories since Project module Stage 2, but the reverse was never done: Quotation's own
  sales-facing `LineItemsEditor.tsx` (the only caller of the shared `ProductPickerModal` in the
  Quotation module) passed its full, unfiltered `products` list straight through, so a salesperson
  building a customer quote could see and pick nuts/screws/consumables never meant to be quoted.
  Fixed by adding a `quotationProducts` filter in `LineItemsEditor.tsx` that excludes any product
  whose category name is in the existing `MATERIAL_CATEGORY_NAMES` constant
  (`src/lib/materialRequisition.ts`) — reusing the same constant Material Requisition/Purchase
  Request already filter *to*, just inverted, rather than inventing a second source of truth for
  which categories are internal-only. **Known limitation, by design**: this is a category-level
  filter, not a per-product flag — a future customer-facing product added to one of the 4 internal
  categories, or an internal-only product added to an existing customer-facing category, will not be
  caught and would need revisiting then. Scoped narrowly: only `LineItemsEditor.tsx`'s picker is
  filtered (Quotation's actual line-item entry point) — `App.tsx`'s own shared `products`/`categories`
  state (also used by the Products admin page and Quotation Templates) is untouched, and Material
  Requisition/Purchase Request's own pickers are unaffected since they fetch their own product lists
  independently. See CHANGELOG.md.
- [x] **[2026-08-20] Resolved the "ใบส่งมอบงาน" document coordination risk — confirmed to just BE the
  pre-existing Delivery Order module (FM-SL-05), not a separate document.** The original coordination
  note (first flagged in `docs/CLAUDE.md`, tracked here as a High Priority item since 2026-08-19)
  worried that a 4th Project-module document type, Work Handover Note, was still needed and unbuilt.
  Direct confirmation from two people on the business side settled it: no separate document is
  needed. The Work Handover Note module built 2026-08-19 to fill this gap (a first-draft, unverified
  structure with no reference PDF, `documentCode: null`) was removed the same day it was confirmed
  redundant — see the 2026-08-20 CHANGELOG.md entry and [MODULES/Project.md](./MODULES/Project.md)
  "Work Handover Note — removed 2026-08-20" for the full removal writeup. **One real gap surfaced
  along the way, not yet addressed**: Delivery Order's print document only ever prints a blank
  signature line for wet-ink signing — it does not capture/embed a real digital customer signature
  the way the now-removed Work Handover Note did via `SignaturePad`. Worth a future pass if digital
  signature capture on delivery documents becomes a real requirement.

- [x] **[2026-08-20] Home-screen icon ("Add to Home Screen") — no app store needed.** Direct request
  for a phone home-screen icon that opens the app without App Store/Play Store distribution. Added a
  Web App Manifest + generated icon set (navy background, re-composited from the existing logo) +
  matching `index.html` meta tags. Deliberately no service worker/offline caching, given this is a
  live, frequently-updated internal ERP. `npm run build` confirmed the manifest/icons land in `dist/`.
  See [ARCHITECTURE.md](./ARCHITECTURE.md) "Home-screen icon" and CHANGELOG.md 2026-08-20c. Not yet
  verified on a real phone (no device in this environment) — worth a real Android + iOS check.

- [x] **[2026-08-20] วางบิลตามงาน becomes a create flow — no more auto-pulled job table.** Direct
  owner request: every accounting document should be click-to-create-yourself, never auto-pulled,
  because a job appearing in a list isn't necessarily billable yet (still waiting on the customer's
  PO, or the customer's documents aren't complete). Replaced `AccountingPage.tsx`'s always-rendered
  Scope of Work table with a "+ สร้างวางบิล" button opening a search-and-pick
  `ScopeOfWorkPickerDialog`; the job list now fetches only when that dialog opens. Clarified with the
  owner first (`AskUserQuestion`) that this should replace the auto-list entirely and that the
  existing PO-copy/delivery-note issuing checklist stays mandatory. `tsc`/`lint`/`build`/`test` clean.
  See [MODULES/Accounting.md](./MODULES/Accounting.md) "2026-08-20 — วางบิลตามงาน becomes a create
  flow" and CHANGELOG.md 2026-08-20a.

- [x] **[2026-08-20] RE page gains its own "+ ออกใบเสร็จ" create entry point.** Follow-up: owner asked
  why the BI list page has no create button, referencing how other ERPs (Odoo) let you pick a source
  document to generate a new one. Researched Odoo's real pattern first: the action lives on the
  *source* document (SO → "Create Invoice", Invoice → "Register Payment"), not as a picker on the
  target's create button — already implemented here for receipts via the per-row "ออกใบเสร็จ" button on
  every AR/IV list row since Phase 1.5. BI can't fit the pattern (always issued together with its
  AR/IV, never standalone), but the RE page itself had no equivalent entry point — added a
  "+ ออกใบเสร็จ" button + search-and-pick dialog there, reusing the existing `issueArReceipt()` flow, no
  backend changes. `tsc`/`lint`/`build`/accounting tests clean. See
  [MODULES/Accounting.md](./MODULES/Accounting.md) and CHANGELOG.md 2026-08-20b.

- [x] **Audit integrity + workflow gap fix pass (2026-07-10, fifth pass)**: responds to the independent Codex re-review addendum's Critical/High findings. Quotation audit-log entries (Created/Updated/Submitted/Approved/Rejected/Status Changed) are now written server-side by `api/handlers/quotes.ts` itself (`writeQuoteAuditEntry()`, identity from the authenticated session only) instead of by the client calling the generic `POST /api/audit-log` — which any authenticated caller could previously forge, corrupting the Dashboard's Sales Activity Analytics. `POST /api/audit-log` now rejects the `"ใบเสนอราคา"` module outright. Reject/Customer-Reject/Cancel workflow actions now require a non-empty comment server-side, not just in the UI. Added the 3 missing workflow notifications (Won/Lost/Cancelled) that a creator previously never received. `tsc`/`lint`/`build` clean; a `vercel dev` browser check confirmed no new console errors (same pre-existing MongoDB DNS limitation, reproduced a fourth time) and surfaced one unrelated pre-existing gap (`App.tsx`'s stuck loading spinner on session-fetch failure, logged above, not fixed this pass). See [CODEX_REVIEW_REPORT.md](./CODEX_REVIEW_REPORT.md) "Claude Fix Status — Addendum Follow-up," [CHANGELOG.md](./CHANGELOG.md), [MODULES/Quotation.md](./MODULES/Quotation.md), [MODULES/Notifications.md](./MODULES/Notifications.md), [MODULES/AuditLog.md](./MODULES/AuditLog.md).
